\widehat{\kappa}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}

\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}(\widehat{\kappa}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}-\kappa_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{2}\geq\frac{1}{\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\Psi_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}+s^{-2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,,

where

\Psi_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}=\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})y^{2}_{\mathchoice{l-1}{l-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l-1$}}}

and

J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\int^{\ln n}_{\mathchoice{-{\ln n}}{-{\ln n}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\ln n}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\ln n}$}}}\,(\dot{\rho}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(t))^{2}/\rho_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(t)\mathrm{d}t
.
Therefore, the Bayes risk can be estimated from below as

\widetilde{{\cal R}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}(\widehat{{\bf h}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})\,\geq\,\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,\frac{1}{\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\Psi_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}+s^{-2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}=\frac{b-a}{n}\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,\frac{1}{\overline{\Psi}_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}+(s^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{-1}J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,,

where

\overline{\Psi}_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}=\frac{(b-a)}{n}\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\Psi_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}=\frac{(b-a)}{n}\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y^{2}_{\mathchoice{l-1}{l-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l-1$}}}\,.

(7.9)

Note here, that in view of Lemmas
A.1
and
A.2

for any
0<\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}<1
and sufficiently large
n
we have
J_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq 1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}
and

\max_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq d}{1\leq j\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}}\overline{\Psi}_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}\leq 1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}
, therefore,

\widetilde{{\cal R}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}(\widehat{{\bf h}})\,\geq\,\frac{b-a}{(1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})n}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,\frac{s^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{1+s^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}=\,\frac{b-a}{(1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})n}\,\sum_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\,\left(1-\frac{j^{\bf k}}{d^{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}\right)\,.

Using here that

\lim_{\mathchoice{d\to\infty}{d\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d\to\infty$}}}d^{-1}\sum_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}^{d}\,\left(1-j^{{\bf k}}d^{-{\bf k}}\right)=\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}(1-t^{{\bf k}})\mathrm{d}t={\bf k}/({\bf k}+1)
,
we obtain for sufficiently large
n

\widetilde{{\cal R}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}(\widehat{{\bf h}})\,\geq\,\frac{(b-a)(1-\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{(1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})n}\,\frac{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}+1}\,.

Therefore, taking into account this in
(
7.8
)
we conclude
through the definition (
7.4
)
and
Lemma
A.3
, that for any
0<\rho
and
\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}<1

\liminf_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\inf_{\mathchoice{\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\in\Xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\in\Xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\in\Xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\in\Xi_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}$}}}\,n^{\frac{2k}{2k+1}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}\,\upsilon(S)\,{\cal R}^{*}(\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}},S)\geq\,\frac{\rho^{\frac{1}{2k+1}}(1-\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})}{1+\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\,{\bf l}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}({\bf r})\,.

Taking here limit as
\rho\to 1
and
\rho_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\to 0
we come to the Theorem
4.1
.

∎

8
Upper bound

We start with the estimation problem
for the functions
S
from
{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}
with known
parameters
k
,
r
and
\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
defined in (
4.2
).
In this case we use the estimator from family (
3.19
)

\widetilde{S}=\widehat{S}_{\mathchoice{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad\widetilde{\alpha}=({\bf k},\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{n})\,,

(8.1)

where

\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\left[\overline{r}(S)/\varepsilon\right]\varepsilon
,

\overline{r}(S)={\bf r}/\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
and
\varepsilon=1/\ln n
.
Note that for sufficiently large
n
,
the parameter

\widetilde{\alpha}
belongs to the set (
3.16
).
In this section we study the risk for the estimator (
8.1
). To this end we need firstly to analyse the
asymptotic behavior of the sequance

\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}+\frac{\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{n}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\,.

(8.2)

Proposition 8.1
.

The sequence
\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)
is bounded from above

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}\,n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\,\upsilon(S)\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)\,\leq\,{\bf l}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}({\bf r})\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}=2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)\,.

Proof.
First, note that

0<\epsilon^{2}(b-a)\leq\inf_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\leq\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\leq b-a
.
This implies directly that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\left|\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}/\overline{r}(S)-1\right|=0\,,

(8.3)

where
\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\left[\overline{r}(S)/\varepsilon\right]\varepsilon
and
\overline{r}(S)={\bf r}/\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
.
Moreover, note that

n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\upsilon(S)\,\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)\leq n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\,\upsilon(S)\,{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+\,\frac{(\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}})^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}}{(b-a)^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}n^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)

and

{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sum^{d}_{j=1}\,(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}={\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}+{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}
,
where

{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}=\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}\,(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}=\sum_{j=[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}^{d}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,.

Remind, that

\widetilde{\omega}=\widetilde{\omega}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}+(b-a)^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\left(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,n\right)^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}

and

\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}=({\bf k}+1)(2{\bf k}+1)/(\pi^{2{\bf k}}{\bf k})
.
Note now, that
Lemma
A.5

and Lemma
A.6

yield

{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}\leq(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\,\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}\,(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+4{\bf r}(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})\,\frac{(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}\widetilde{\omega}}{d^{2k}}

and

{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\leq(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+{\bf r}(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})\frac{(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}}{d^{2}\,\widetilde{\omega}^{2(k-1)}}\,,

i.e.

{\bf G}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}){\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+4{\bf r}(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})\,\widetilde{{\bf G}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
,
where

{\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq 1}{j\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq 1$}}}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\sum_{\mathchoice{j\leq\widetilde{\omega}}{j\leq\widetilde{\omega}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\leq\widetilde{\omega}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\leq\widetilde{\omega}$}}}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+\sum_{\mathchoice{j>\widetilde{\omega}}{j>\widetilde{\omega}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j>\widetilde{\omega}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j>\widetilde{\omega}$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}:={\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}+{\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}

and

\widetilde{{\bf G}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\widetilde{\omega}\,d^{-2{\bf k}}+d^{-2}\widetilde{\omega}^{-2({\bf k}-1)}
.
Note, that

n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\upsilon(S){\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}=\frac{\upsilon(S)}{(b-a)^{2{\bf k}{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}\,\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}\,j^{2{\bf k}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\\
\leq\frac{{\bf u}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\widetilde{{\bf t}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}\,{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,

where
{\bf u}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sup_{\mathchoice{j\geq j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j\geq j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}\,j^{2k}/((b-a)^{2{\bf k}}{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})
. It is clear that
\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,{\bf u}^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\pi^{-2k}
.
Therefore, from
(
8.3
) it follows that

\displaystyle\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\frac{n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\upsilon(S)\,{\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}}{\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}\,{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\leq\pi^{-2{\bf k}}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}{\bf r})^{-{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\,.

Next note, that
for any
0<\widetilde{\varepsilon}<1

and for sufficiently large
n

\displaystyle{\bf G}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\leq\frac{1}{{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}}}\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}}\,{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\leq\frac{(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})((b-a)\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}})^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}{\pi^{2{\bf k}}\,(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}{\bf r}n)^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}}\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}}{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,,

i.e.

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\frac{n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\upsilon(S)\,\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq[\widetilde{\omega}]+1$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}{\sum_{\mathchoice{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=j_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}$}}}^{[\widetilde{\omega}]}{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}}\leq\pi^{-2{\bf k}}(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}{\bf r})^{-{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\,.

Next, since
\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)/\widetilde{\omega}\to{\bf k}{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}/({\bf k}+1)
as
n\to\infty
,
we get directly that

\lim_{\mathchoice{T\to\infty}{T\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle T\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle T\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\left|\frac{(\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}})^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)}{(b-a)^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}n^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}}-\frac{(\varpi_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}{\bf r})^{1/(2{\bf k}+1)}{\bf k}{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}{({\bf k}+1)}\right|=0\,.

Finally, taking into account, that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}n^{{\bf k}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}}\widetilde{{\bf G}}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=0
,
we obtain
Proposition
8.1
.

∎

Theorem 8.1
.

The estimator
\widetilde{S}
constructed on the trigonometric basis
satisfies the following asymptotic upper bound

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}\upsilon(S)\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\|\widetilde{S}-S\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma}{\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma$}}}\,\leq{\bf l}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k}}{{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k}$}}}({\bf r})\,.

(8.4)

Proof.
We denote
\widetilde{\lambda}=\lambda_{\mathchoice{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}}

and
\widetilde{\omega}=\omega_{\mathchoice{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\widetilde{\alpha}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\widetilde{\alpha}$}}}
.
Now we recall that the Fourier coefficients
on the set
\Gamma

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,=\,\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,+\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\quad\mbox{with}\quad\zeta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=\sqrt{\frac{b-a}{d}}\eta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}+\varpi_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,.

Therefore, on the set
\Gamma

we can represent the empiric squared error as

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{S}-S\|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}-2M_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}

\displaystyle-2\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,(1\,-\,\widetilde{\lambda}(j))\,\widetilde{\lambda}(j)\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,\varpi_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}+\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\,\zeta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,,

where

M_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sqrt{b-a}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,(1\,-\,\widetilde{\lambda}(j))\,\widetilde{\lambda}(j)\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,\eta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}/\sqrt{d}
.
Now for any
0<\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}<1

\displaystyle 2\left|\sum_{j=1}^{d}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))\widetilde{\lambda}(j)\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,\varpi_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\right|\,

\displaystyle\leq\,\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}^{-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\varpi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}

\displaystyle\leq x\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,+\frac{\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}n}\,,

where
\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
is defined in (
3.6
).
Therefore,

\|\widetilde{S}-S\|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}^{2}\,\leq\,(1+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\sum_{j=1}^{d}(1-\widetilde{\lambda}(j))^{2}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}-2M_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+\frac{\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}n}+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\zeta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,.

By the same way we get

\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\,\zeta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\leq\,\frac{(1+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})(b-a)}{d}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\,\eta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}+(1+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}^{-1})\frac{\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{n}\,.

Thus, on the set
\Gamma
we find that for any
0<\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}<1

\|\widetilde{S}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-S\|_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}^{2}\leq(1+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)-2M_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+(1+\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}){\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}+\frac{3\varpi^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}{\varepsilon_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}n}\,,

where
\Upsilon_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}(S)
is defined in (
8.2
)
and

{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\frac{1}{d^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\left(d(b-a)\eta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}-\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right)\,.

(8.5)

We recall that the variance
\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}
is defined in (
4.2
).
In view of Lemma
A.7

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,M^{2}_{n}\leq\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}(b-a)}{d}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}(b-a)}{d}\|S\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\leq\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}(b-a)^{2}}{d}\,,

where
\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}
is given in
(
2.16
). Moreover, using that
{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,M_{n}=0
, we get

|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,M_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma}{\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma$}}}|\,=\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,M_{n}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma^{c}}{\Gamma^{c}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}}|\,\leq(b-a)\sqrt{\frac{\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}(\Gamma^{c})}{d}}\,.

Therefore, Proposition
2.2

yields

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,M_{n}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma}{\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma$}}}|=0\,.

(8.6)

Now, the property
(
3.7
)
Proposition
8.1

and
Lemma
A.8
imply the inequality (
8.4
).
Hence Theorem
8.1
.

∎

It is clear, that
Theorem
3.1
and Theorem
8.1

imply Theorem
4.2
.
