A
Appendix

A.1
Properties of the prior distribution
7.2

In this section we study properties of the distribution used in
(
7.7
).

Lemma A.1
.

For any
{\bf N}>2
there exists a continuously differentiable probability density
\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(\cdot)

on
{\mathbb{R}}

with the support on the interval
[-{\bf N}\,,\,{\bf N}]
, i.e.
\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)>0
for
-{\bf N}<z<{\bf N}
and

\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=0
for
|z|\geq{\bf N}
,
such that for any
{\bf N}>2
the integral

\int^{{\bf N}}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}z\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z=0
and, moreover,

\int^{{\bf N}}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}z^{2}\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z\to 1

and

J_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}=\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}}{{\mathbb{R}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}}\,(\dot{\rho}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z))^{2}/\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\,\mathrm{d}z\to 1

as
{\bf N}\to\infty
.

Proof.
First we set

V(z)=\left(\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{-1}{-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}\,e^{-\frac{1}{1-t^{2}}}\mathrm{d}t\right)^{-1}\,e^{-\frac{1}{1-z^{2}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|z|\leq 1\}}{\{|z|\leq 1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|z|\leq 1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|z|\leq 1\}$}}}
.
It is clear that this function is infinitely times continuously differentiable, such that

V(z)>0
for
|z|<1
,

V(z)=0
for
|z|\geq 1
and
\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{-1}{-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}V(z)\mathrm{d}z=1
.
Now for
{\bf N}\geq 2
we set

\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{-1}{-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|z+u|\leq N-1\}}{\{|z+u|\leq N-1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|z+u|\leq N-1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|z+u|\leq N-1\}$}}}\,V(u)\mathrm{d}u=\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}}{{\mathbb{R}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|t|\leq N-1\}}{\{|t|\leq N-1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|t|\leq N-1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|t|\leq N-1\}$}}}\,V(t-z)\mathrm{d}t
.
Using here the properties of the function
V
we can obtain directly that

\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(-z)
for
z\in{\mathbb{R}}
,

\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=1
for
|z|\leq{\bf N}-2
,
\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)>0
for
{\bf N}-2<|z|<{\bf N}
and

\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=0
for
|z|\geq{\bf N}
. Moreover, it is clear that the derivative

\dot{\chi}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=-\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}}{{\mathbb{R}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|t|\leq N-1\}}{\{|t|\leq N-1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|t|\leq N-1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|t|\leq N-1\}$}}}\,\dot{V}(t-z)\mathrm{d}t=-\int^{1}_{\mathchoice{-1}{-1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-1$}}}\,{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\{|u+z|\leq N-1\}}{\{|u+z|\leq N-1\}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\{|u+z|\leq N-1\}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\{|u+z|\leq N-1\}$}}}\,\dot{V}(u)\mathrm{d}u
.
Note here that

|\dot{V}(z)|\leq{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\sqrt{V(z)}
for some

{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}>0
.
Now through the
Bunyakovsky - Cauchy - Schwartz inequality we get that

\dot{\chi}^{2}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\leq 2{\bf c}_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\quad\mbox{for}\quad|z|<{\bf N}
.
Now we set

\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=\left(\int^{N}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}\varphi(t)\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(t)\mathrm{d}t\right)^{-1}\varphi(z)\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)
, where
\varphi(z)
the
(0,1)
Gaussian density,
It is clear that
\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)
is the the continuously differentiable probability density with the support
[-{\bf N}\,,\,{\bf N}]

such that for any
{\bf N}
the integral

\int^{{\bf N}}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}z\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z=0

and
\int^{N}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}\varphi(t)\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(t)\mathrm{d}t\to 1
,

\int^{{\bf N}}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}z^{2}\rho_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z\to 1

for
{\bf N}\to\infty
.
Moreover, the Fisher information can be represented as

J_{\mathchoice{N}{N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N$}}}=\left(\int^{{\bf N}}_{\mathchoice{-{\bf N}}{-{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-{\bf N}$}}}\varphi(t)\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(t)\mathrm{d}t\right)^{-1}\left(\int_{\mathchoice{{\mathbb{R}}}{{\mathbb{R}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\mathbb{R}}$}}}\frac{\dot{\varphi}^{2}(z)}{\varphi(z)}\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z+\Delta_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}\right)\,,

where, taking into account that
\dot{\chi}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)=0
for
|z|\leq{\bf N}-2
,

\Delta_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}=2\int_{\mathchoice{|z|\geq{\bf N}-2}{|z|\geq{\bf N}-2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|z|\geq{\bf N}-2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|z|\geq{\bf N}-2$}}}\dot{\varphi}(z)\dot{\chi}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)\mathrm{d}z+\int_{\mathchoice{|z|\geq{\bf N}-2}{|z|\geq{\bf N}-2}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle|z|\geq{\bf N}-2$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle|z|\geq{\bf N}-2$}}}\,\varphi(z)\frac{\dot{\chi}^{2}_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)}{\chi_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}(z)}\mathrm{d}z\,.

Therefore,
\Delta_{\mathchoice{{\bf N}}{{\bf N}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf N}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf N}$}}}\to 0
as
{\bf N}\to\infty
.
Hence Lemma
A.1
.

∎

Lemma A.2
.

The term (
7.9
) is such that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\max_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq d}{1\leq j\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}}\,|\overline{\Psi}_{\mathchoice{n,j}{n,j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n,j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n,j$}}}-1|=0
.

Proof.
First, note that

y_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}=y_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\prod^{l}_{\mathchoice{i=1}{i=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i=1$}}}\,S_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})+\sum^{l}_{\mathchoice{\iota=1}{\iota=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\iota=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\iota=1$}}}\,\prod^{l}_{\mathchoice{i=\iota+1}{i=\iota+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i=\iota+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i=\iota+1$}}}\,S_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})\xi_{\mathchoice{\iota}{\iota}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\iota$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\iota$}}}
for
l\geq 1
.
Therefore,

\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}=y^{2}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\prod^{l}_{\mathchoice{i=1}{i=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i=1$}}}\,S^{2}_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})+\sum^{l-1}_{\mathchoice{\iota=1}{\iota=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\iota=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\iota=1$}}}\,\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,\prod^{l}_{\mathchoice{i=\iota+1}{i=\iota+1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i=\iota+1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i=\iota+1$}}}\,S^{2}_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{i}{i}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle i$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle i$}}})+1

and due to (
7.5
) we obtain that
for any
n\geq 1
for which
\delta^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}<1

\sup_{\mathchoice{l\geq 1}{l\geq 1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l\geq 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l\geq 1$}}}|\widetilde{{\bf E}}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}\,y^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}-1|\leq(\delta^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})^{2}\frac{\left(y^{2}_{\mathchoice{0}{0}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0$}}}+1\right)}{1-(\delta^{*}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})^{2}}\to 0\quad\mbox{as}\quad n\to\infty\,.

Since,

(b-a)\sum^{n}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})=n
,
we get Lemma

A.2
.

∎

Lemma A.3
.

The term
{\bf R}_{\mathchoice{0,n}{0,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0,n$}}}
in (
7.8
) is such that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{{\bf b}}\,{\bf R}_{\mathchoice{0,n}{0,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0,n$}}}=0

for any
{\bf b}>0
and
0<\rho<1
.

Proof.
First note, that taking into account in the definition of term
{\bf R}_{\mathchoice{0,n}{0,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0,n$}}}

in (
7.8
), that
|\eta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\leq\ln n
,
we get that

{\bf R}_{\mathchoice{0,n}{0,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 0,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 0,n$}}}\leq\left({\bf r}+\ln^{2}n\,\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,s^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)\,\mu_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}({\bf D}^{c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})
.
Therefore, to show this lemma it suffices to check that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{{\bf b}}\,\mu_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}({\bf D}^{c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})=0
for any
{\bf b}>0
.
To do this note, that the definition of
{\bf D}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
in
(
7.6
) implies

\mu_{\mathchoice{\kappa}{\kappa}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kappa$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kappa$}}}({\bf D}^{c}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}})\leq{\bf P}(\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}>{\bf r})

and

\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\sum^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\kappa^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
. So, it suffices to show that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{{\bf b}}\,{\bf P}(\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}>{\bf r})=0\quad\mbox{for any}\quad{\bf b}>0\,.

(A.1)

Indeed, first note, that the definition (
7.2
) through Lemma
A.1

and the property
(
7.3
)
imply
directly

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,{\bf E}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}s^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}{\bf E}\,(\eta^{*}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})^{2}=\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}s^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=\rho{\bf r}\,.

Setting now

\widetilde{\zeta}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}-{\bf E}\,\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=(b-a)\sum^{d_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,s^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\widetilde{\eta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}/n

and

\widetilde{\eta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}=(\eta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{2}-{\bf E}(\eta^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{2}
,
we get for large
n
that

\left\{\zeta_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}>{\bf r}\right\}\subset\left\{\widetilde{\zeta}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}>{\bf r}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}\right\}

for
{\bf r}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}}={\bf r}(1-\rho)/2
.
Now the correlation inequality from

[
12
]

and the bound
|\widetilde{\eta}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}|\leq 2\ln^{2}n
imply
that for any
p\geq 2
there exists some constant
C_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}>0
for which

{\bf E}\,\widetilde{\zeta}^{p}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\leq C_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}\frac{(\ln n)^{2p}}{n^{p}}\,\left(\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,(s^{*}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})^{2}\,{\bf a}^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\right)^{p/2}\leq C_{\mathchoice{p}{p}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle p$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle p$}}}\,n^{-\frac{p}{4{\bf k}+2}}\,(\ln n)^{2p}\,,

i.e. the expectation

{\bf E}\,\widetilde{\zeta}^{p}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
and, therefore,

n^{{\bf b}}{\bf P}(\widetilde{\zeta}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}>{\bf r}_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}})\to 0

as

n\to\infty
for any
{\bf b}>0
.
This implies (
A.1
) and, hence Lemma
A.3
.

∎

A.2
Properties of the trigonometric basis.

First we need the following lemma from

[
17
]
.

Lemma A.4
.

Let
f
be an absolutely continuous function,
f:[a,b]\to{\mathbb{R}},
with

\|\dot{f}\|<\infty
and
g
be a piecewise constant function
[a,b]\to{\mathbb{R}}
of a form

g(x)=\sum_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}^{d}\,c_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,\chi_{\mathchoice{(z_{\mathchoice{j-1}{j-1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j-1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j-1$}}},z_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}]}{(z_{\mathchoice{j-1}{j-1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j-1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j-1$}}},z_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}]}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle(z_{\mathchoice{j-1}{j-1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j-1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j-1$}}},z_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}]$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle(z_{\mathchoice{j-1}{j-1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j-1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j-1$}}},z_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}]$}}}(x)

where
c_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}
are some constants. Then for any
\varepsilon>0,
the function
\Delta=f-g

satisfies the following inequalities

\|\Delta\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\leq(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\|\Delta\|^{2}+(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})\frac{(b-a)^{2}\|\dot{f}\|^{2}}{d^{2}}\,.

Lemma A.5
.

For any
1\leq j\leq d

the trigonometric Fourier coefficients
(\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq d}{1\leq j\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}}

for the functions
S
from
the class
{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}
with
{\bf k}\geq 1
satisfy, for any
\widetilde{\varepsilon}>0
,
the following inequality

\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,\leq\,(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,+4{\bf r}(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}d^{-2k}
.

Proof.
First we represent the function
S
as

S(x)=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\,\phi_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(x)+\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(x)
and

\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}(x)=\sum_{\mathchoice{l>d}{l>d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l>d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l>d$}}}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\,\phi_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}(x)
, i.e.

\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=(S,\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}=\theta_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+(\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}},\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}})_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}

and, therefore,
\forall\widetilde{\varepsilon}>0
we get

\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\leq(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}+(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})\|\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}
.
Lemma
A.4
with
g=0
implies

\|\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\leq 2\|\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}+2(b-a)^{2}d^{-2}\|\dot{\Delta}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}
.
Using here that

2\pi[l/2]\geq l
for
l\geq 2
,
we obtain that

\|\Delta_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}=\sum_{\mathchoice{l>d}{l>d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l>d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l>d$}}}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\leq{\bf r}/{\bf a}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\leq(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}{\bf r}d^{-2{\bf k}}

and

\|\dot{\Delta}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\|^{2}=(2\pi/(b-a))^{2}\sum_{\mathchoice{l>d}{l>d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l>d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l>d$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\,[l/2]^{2}\leq(b-a)^{2({\bf k}-1)}d^{-2({\bf k}-1)}\,.

(A.2)

Hence Lemma
A.5

∎

Lemma A.6
.

For any
d\geq 2
and
1\leq N\leq d

the coefficients
(\theta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq d}{1\leq j\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}}

of functions
S
from
the class
{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf r},{\bf k}}{{\bf r},{\bf k}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf r},{\bf k}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf r},{\bf k}$}}}
with
{\bf k}\geq 1
satisfy, for any
\widetilde{\varepsilon}>0
,
the inequality

\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=N}{j=N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=N$}}}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,\leq\,(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon})\,\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq N}{j\geq N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq N$}}}\,\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\,+(1+\widetilde{\varepsilon}^{-1})(b-a)^{2{\bf k}}{\bf r}\,d^{-2}N^{-2({\bf k}-1)}
.

Proof.
Note that

\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=N}{j=N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=N$}}}\theta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=\min_{\mathchoice{x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}},\ldots,x_{\mathchoice{N-1}{N-1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N-1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N-1$}}}}{x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}},\ldots,x_{\mathchoice{N-1}{N-1}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N-1$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N-1$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}},\ldots,x_{\mathchoice{N-1}{N-1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N-1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N-1$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle x_{\mathchoice{1}{1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1$}}},\ldots,x_{\mathchoice{N-1}{N-1}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N-1$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N-1$}}}$}}}\,\|S-\sum^{N-1}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,x_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}\leq\|\Delta_{\mathchoice{N}{N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N$}}}\|^{2}_{\mathchoice{d}{d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle d$}}}

and
\Delta_{\mathchoice{N}{N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle N$}}}(t)=\sum_{\mathchoice{j\geq N}{j\geq N}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j\geq N$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j\geq N$}}}\,\theta_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(t)
.
Lemma
A.4
and
(
A.2
),
imply Lemma
A.6

∎

A.3
Technical lemmas

Lemma A.7
.

For any non random coefficients
({\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}})_{\mathchoice{1\leq j\leq d}{1\leq j\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq j\leq d$}}}

{\bf E}\left(\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\eta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\right)^{2}\leq\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\,{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}^{2}\,,

where the coefficient
\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}
is given in (
2.16
).

Proof.
Using the definition of
\eta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}
in (
3.5
) and the bounds (
2.16
),
we get

\displaystyle{\bf E}\left(\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\eta_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{b-a}{d}{\bf E}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\sigma^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\left(\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}\frac{b-a}{d}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\left(\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf u}_{\mathchoice{j,l}{j,l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,l$}}}\phi_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})\right)^{2}\,.

Now, the orthonormality property (
3.2
)
implies this lemma.

∎

Lemma A.8
.

For the sequence (
8.5
) the following limit property holds true

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma}{\Gamma}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma$}}}|=0\,.

Proof.
First of all, note that, using the definition of
{\bf s}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}
in (
3.11
), we obtain

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,\eta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}={\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf s}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=\frac{1}{d}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,\frac{1}{H_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}}+\frac{1}{d}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\overline{{\bf s}}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,,

where

\overline{{\bf s}}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,\sigma^{2}_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}\overline{\phi}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(x_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})
and

\overline{\phi}_{j}(z)=(b-a)\phi^{2}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z)-1
.
Therefore, we can represent the expectation of
{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}
as

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}=\frac{\|\widetilde{\lambda}\|^{2}}{d^{2}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}+\frac{b-a}{d^{2}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\,,

where
\|\widetilde{\lambda}\|^{2}=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)
,

{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}=\frac{b-a}{d}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,\frac{d}{H_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}}-\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\quad\mbox{and}\quad{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}=\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\overline{{\bf s}}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\,.

Note now, that using
Proposition
2.12

and the dominated convergence theorem
in
the definition
(
2.6
)
we obtain that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\max_{\mathchoice{1\leq l\leq d}{1\leq l\leq d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1\leq l\leq d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1\leq l\leq d$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,\left|\frac{d}{H_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}}-(1-S^{2}(z_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}))\right|=0\,.

Taking into account that for the functions from the class
(
2.4
) their derivatives are uniformly bounded, we can deduce that

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\left|\frac{b-a}{d}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{l=1}{l=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l=1$}}}\,(1-S^{2}(z_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}}))-\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}\right|=0\,,

i.e.

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{1,n}{1,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,n$}}}|=0
.
Therefore, taking into account that

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\frac{\|\widetilde{\lambda}\|^{2}}{d^{2}}<\infty\,,

we obtain that

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|\leq(b-a)\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,{\bf U}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\,,

(A.3)

where

{\bf U}^{*}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}=\frac{n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}}{d^{2}}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}|\,.

Now, using Lemma A.2 from
[
9
]
we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\right|\,

\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{l=1}^{d}\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,\sigma^{2}_{l}\,\sum_{j=1}^{d}\,\widetilde{\lambda}^{2}(j)\,\overline{\phi}_{\mathchoice{j}{j}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j$}}}(z_{\mathchoice{l}{l}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle l$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle l$}}})\right|

\displaystyle\leq\,d\,\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}\,(2^{2{\bf k}+1}+2^{{\bf k}+2}+1)\leq\,5\,d\,\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}\,2^{2{\bf k}}\,.

The definition of
\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}
in
(
2.16
) implies

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,d\,\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}<\infty
,
i.e.

\limsup_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}\left|\,{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{2,n}{2,n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 2,n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 2,n$}}}\right|\,<\infty\,.

Therefore, the using this bound in (
A.3
)
implies

\overline{\lim}_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in\Theta_{\mathchoice{\varepsilon,L}{\varepsilon,L}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\varepsilon,L$}}}$}}}\,\sup_{\mathchoice{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{{\bf p}\in{\cal P}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p}\in{\cal P}$}}}\,|{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\,{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|=0\,.

Using the inequality (A.4) from
[
4
]

we get

{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\eta^{4}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}\leq 64{\bf v}^{*}\sigma^{2}_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}
,
where the coefficient
{\bf v}^{*}
is given in (
2.15
). From this we obtain, that

\displaystyle{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}|{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma^{c}}{\Gamma^{c}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{(b-a)}{d}\sum^{d}_{\mathchoice{j=1}{j=1}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j=1$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j=1$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}\eta^{2}_{\mathchoice{j,d}{j,d}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle j,d$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle j,d$}}}{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma^{c}}{\Gamma^{c}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}}+\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}(\Gamma^{c})

\displaystyle\leq\frac{8\sigma_{\mathchoice{1,*}{1,*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle 1,*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle 1,*$}}}(b-a)\sqrt{{\bf v}^{*}}}{d}\sqrt{{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}(\Gamma^{c})}+\varsigma_{\mathchoice{*}{*}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle*$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle*$}}}{\bf P}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}(\Gamma^{c})\,.

So, Proposition
2.2

implies

\lim_{\mathchoice{n\to\infty}{n\to\infty}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n\to\infty$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n\to\infty$}}}\,n^{2{\bf k}/(2{\bf k}+1)}\sup_{\mathchoice{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.5382pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle S\in{\cal W}_{\mathchoice{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{{\bf k},{\bf r}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}{\lower 0.37672pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf k},{\bf r}$}}}$}}}{\bf E}_{\mathchoice{{\bf p},S}{{\bf p},S}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle{\bf p},S$}}}|{\bf U}_{\mathchoice{n}{n}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle n$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle n$}}}|{\bf 1}_{\mathchoice{\Gamma^{c}}{\Gamma^{c}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}{\lower 0.75346pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\Gamma^{c}$}}}=0
.
Hence
Lemma
A.8
.

∎
